\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
Contains {\itshape low-\/level} functions and variables of the \textbackslash{}\+CImg Library. 

Most of the functions and variables within this namespace are used by the \textbackslash{}\+CImg library for low-\/level operations. You may use them to access specific const values or environment variables internally used by \textbackslash{}\+CImg. \begin{DoxyWarning}{Warning}
Never write {\ttfamily using namespace cimg\+\_\+library\+::cimg;} in your source code. Lot of functions in the {\ttfamily cimg\+:\+: namespace} have the same names as standard C functions that may be defined in the global namespace {\ttfamily \+::}. 
\end{DoxyWarning}

Set current \textbackslash{}\+CImg openmp mode. 

The way openmp-\/based methods are handled by \textbackslash{}\+CImg can be changed dynamically, using this function. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em mode} & Desired openmp mode. Possible values are\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\ttfamily 0}\+: Never parallelize.
\item {\ttfamily 1}\+: Always parallelize.
\item {\ttfamily 2}\+: Adaptive parallelization mode (default behavior). 
\end{DoxyItemize}\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

template$<$typename t$>$ \\
int cimg\+\_\+library\+\_\+suffixed\+::cimg\+::dialog (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}const}]{title}{, }\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}const}]{msg}{, }\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}const}]{button1\+\_\+label}{, }\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}const}]{button2\+\_\+label}{, }\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}const}]{button3\+\_\+label}{, }\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}const}]{button4\+\_\+label}{, }\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}const}]{button5\+\_\+label}{, }\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}const}]{button6\+\_\+label}{, }\item[{const \mbox{\hyperlink{structcimg__library__suffixed_1_1CImg}{CImg}}$<$ t $>$ \&}]{logo}{, }\item[{const bool}]{is\+\_\+centered}{ = {\ttfamily false}}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}}



Display a simple dialog box, and wait for the user\textquotesingle{}s response. 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em title} & Title of the dialog window. \\
\hline
{\em msg} & Main message displayed inside the dialog window. \\
\hline
{\em button1\+\_\+label} & Label of the 1st button. \\
\hline
{\em button2\+\_\+label} & Label of the 2nd button ({\ttfamily 0} to hide button). \\
\hline
{\em button3\+\_\+label} & Label of the 3rd button ({\ttfamily 0} to hide button). \\
\hline
{\em button4\+\_\+label} & Label of the 4th button ({\ttfamily 0} to hide button). \\
\hline
{\em button5\+\_\+label} & Label of the 5th button ({\ttfamily 0} to hide button). \\
\hline
{\em button6\+\_\+label} & Label of the 6th button ({\ttfamily 0} to hide button). \\
\hline
{\em logo} & \doxylink{classImage}{Image} logo displayed at the left of the main message. \\
\hline
{\em is\+\_\+centered} & Tells if the dialog window must be centered on the screen. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
Indice of clicked button (from {\ttfamily 0} to {\ttfamily 5}), or {\ttfamily -\/1} if the dialog window has been closed by the user. 
\end{DoxyReturn}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Up to 6 buttons can be defined in the dialog window.
\item The function returns when a user clicked one of the button or closed the dialog window.
\item If a button text is set to 0, the corresponding button (and the followings) will not appear in the dialog box. At least one button must be specified. 
\end{DoxyItemize}
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{\textcolor{keyword}{const}\ \textcolor{keywordtype}{double}}

Set current \textbackslash{}\+CImg exception mode. 

The way error messages are handled by \textbackslash{}\+CImg can be changed dynamically, using this function. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em mode} & Desired exception mode. Possible values are\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\ttfamily 0}\+: Hide library messages (quiet mode).
\item {\ttfamily 1}\+: Print library messages on the console.
\item {\ttfamily 2}\+: Display library messages on a dialog window.
\item {\ttfamily 3}\+: Do as {\ttfamily 1} + add extra debug warnings (slow down the code!).
\item {\ttfamily 4}\+: Do as {\ttfamily 2} + add extra debug warnings (slow down the code!). 
\end{DoxyItemize}\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacecimg__library__suffixed_1_1cimg_ac9630aa9259053d209c7ff02823efda0} 
template$<$typename T$>$ \\
T cimg\+\_\+library\+\_\+suffixed\+::cimg\+::minmod (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const T \&}]{a}{, }\item[{const T \&}]{b}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}}



Return the min-\/mod of two values. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
{\itshape minmod({\ttfamily a},{\ttfamily b})} is defined to be\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\itshape minmod({\ttfamily a},{\ttfamily b}) = min({\ttfamily a},{\ttfamily b})}, if {\ttfamily a} and {\ttfamily b} have the same sign.
\item {\itshape minmod({\ttfamily a},{\ttfamily b}) = 0}, if {\ttfamily a} and {\ttfamily b} have different signs.

\mbox{\hyperlink{CImg_8h_ae78061fec7a4c85b36265b3fac6ee928}{cimg\+\_\+long}} cimg\+\_\+library\+\_\+suffixed\+::cimg\+::wait (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const unsigned int}]{milliseconds}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}}



Wait for a given number of milliseconds since the last call to wait(). 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em milliseconds} & Number of milliseconds to wait for. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
Number of milliseconds elapsed since the last call to wait(). 
\end{DoxyReturn}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Same as sleep() with a waiting time computed with regard to the last call of wait(). It may be used to temporize your program properly, without wasting CPU time. 
\end{DoxyNote}
\Hypertarget{namespacecimg__library__suffixed_1_1cimg_acfd3624d72ed7f79c82ec45646c685e7}\index{cimg\_library\_suffixed::cimg@{cimg\_library\_suffixed::cimg}!warn@{warn}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacecimg__library__suffixed_1_1cimg_acfd3624d72ed7f79c82ec45646c685e7} 
void cimg\+\_\+library\+\_\+suffixed\+::cimg\+::warn (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}const}]{format}{, }\item[{}]{...}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}}



Display a warning message on the default output stream. 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em format} & C-\/string containing the format of the message, as with {\ttfamily std\+::printf()}. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
If configuration macro {\ttfamily cimg\+\_\+strict\+\_\+warnings} is set, this function throws a {\ttfamily \doxylink{structcimg__library__suffixed_1_1CImgWarningException}{CImg\+Warning\+Exception}} instead. 
\end{DoxyNote}
\begin{DoxyWarning}{Warning}
As the first argument is a format string, it is highly recommended to write 
\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{\mbox{\hyperlink{namespacecimg__library__suffixed_1_1cimg_acfd3624d72ed7f79c82ec45646c685e7}{cimg::warn}}(\textcolor{stringliteral}{"{}\%s"{}},warning\_message);}

\end{DoxyCode}
 instead of 
\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{\mbox{\hyperlink{namespacecimg__library__suffixed_1_1cimg_acfd3624d72ed7f79c82ec45646c685e7}{cimg::warn}}(warning\_message);}

\end{DoxyCode}
 if {\ttfamily warning\+\_\+message} can be arbitrary, to prevent nasty memory access. 
\end{DoxyWarning}
